The police cut off his sacred head, but his body remained standing for two hours until the faithful arrived in all haste from the city, for they were near the prison and were unaware of what had happened until they were informed of it. They took the body of their pastor and their leader, placed it in a shroud, carried it to the city, and placed it on his seat where no one had ever seen him seated. It is said that when they asked him why he did not sit there, he answered that he saw seated there


[p. 359] {#po.3.p359}
يجسس ان! يجلس على ذلك الكرسى ثم4 جعلوا جنب اجسادة القديسين والزمان الذى اقامه على الكرسى احدى عشر سنة صلاته معنا7 امين وفيه استشهد القديس العظيم اقليمنطس بابا رومية هذا القديس كان من اهل رومية من اولاد المملكة فادبانه ابواه وعلماه كل حكمة اليونانية فلما بشر الرسول بظرس فى رومية وسمع هذا القديس بخر وعظم الايات الذى يصنع فاستدعاة الى مجاسه وباحته ماحتة كبيرة فين له الرسول ضلالة عادة الاوثان واثبت عنده الاهية المسيح الذى به يبشروا وباسمه تعمل المعجزات وآمن على يديه وعمده ثم تبعه من ذلك اليوم وكان يكتب سير التلاميذ وما ينالهم من الملوك ثم بشر فى مدن كثيرة وسلمت ” اليه التلاميذ كتب القوانين التى نطقوا بها ثم صار بطريركا على رومية وبشر فيها ورد كثيرين من اهلها الى معرفة السيد المسيح مع به طراسايس فارسل من قبض عليه ولما حض قدامه طالبه بالسجود للاوثان وان يكفر بالمسيح فلما لم بطاوعه انفاه الى بعض المدن
the power of the Lord and that he did not dare to sit there. They placed him next to the bodies of the saints. He remained patriarch for eleven years. May his prayer be with us! Amen. On this day died the martyr the great Saint Clement (Aglimantos), Pope of Rome (Roumyah). This saint was born in Rome of the imperial family. His parents had him instructed and taught him all the wisdom of the Greeks. When the apostle Peter (Botros) preached the Gospel in Rome, Clement, having heard speak of him and of the greatness of the miracles he accomplished, had him come to his dwelling. He felt a lively affection for him. The apostle demonstrated to him the error of idolatry and established in his mind the divinity of the Messiah who was preached and in whose name the miracles were done. Clement believed through him and received baptism. Then he followed him from that day; he wrote the lives of the disciples and what they experienced from the kings. Then he preached in a great number of cities. * The disciples entrusted to him the books of the canons according to which they spoke. Then he became patriarch of Rome, preached there, and brought back many of its inhabitants to the knowledge of Our Lord the Messiah. Trajan (Taräsâis) heard speak of him: he sent to arrest him and when he was in his presence, he asked him to prostrate himself before the idols and to deny the Messiah. As he did not obey him, he banished him to a city, for he feared the


[p. 360] {#po.3.p360}
لانه خاف من اهل المدينة ومن اهله ان يعاقبه عندهم ثم ارسل خلفه رسالة الى المتولى بتلك المدينة بان يتولا عقابه وموته فربط الامير فى عنقه مرسا ثم القالا فى البح فاسلم فيه نفسه ولما كان بعد سنة توارت عنه اللجة فظهر جسد القديس راقد فى قعر البحر كانه حيا ودخلوا الناس وتباركوا منه وقصدوا ان يشيلوه فاستحضروا جرن وجعلوه فيه ثم قصدوا ان يخرجو من اللحر فما قدروا على تحريكه من مكانه فعلموا انه لا يريد الخروج من مكانه وتركوه ومضوا وصارت اللجة تنوارى عنه فى كل سنة فى يوم عيده فتدخل اناس اليه ويتباركوا منه وقد ترددت المسافرين كثيرا وحكوا ذلك وكتب من جملة عجائه ان فى بعض السنين لما دخلوا اليه ليتاركوا منه وعند خروجهم نسوا صبيا صغيرا تخلف خلف الجرن الذى فيه جسد القديس وذلك بتدبير من السيح ليظهر فضل محبته وما الوه من الكرامة ولما انطبقت عليه اللجة فطلبوا ابواه فلم يجدوةه فتحققه انه قد مات واكله الوحش الذى فى البحر فبكوا عليه وعملوا عليه التراحيم والقداسات الجارى بها العادة ولما كان فى السنة الاتية توارت ايضا اللجة ودخات الناس كعادتهم فوجدوا الصبى حيا ألو كيف كان مقامك وما هو الذى كنت تنتدى به فقال
inhabitants of Rome and his family, if he punished him near them. Then he sent, after him, to the governor of this city, a message charging him to see to his punishment and his death. This governor attached an anchor to the neck of the saint and threw him into the sea, where he gave up his soul. When a year had passed, the sea moved away from him and the body of the saint appeared, lying at the bottom as if he were alive. The people entered there and were blessed by him. They formed the project of taking him out; they brought a chest, placed him in it, and wanted to take him out of the sea, but they could not move him from his place. Then they recognized that he refused to leave this place; they left him there and departed. Since then, every year, the sea opens on the day of his feast; the people enter there and are blessed by him. The travelers who have gone there in great number have recounted this. - Among his miracles, it is still written that one year, when the Christians had entered there to be blessed by him, they forgot upon leaving a young child who had remained behind the chest where the body of the saint is: it was according to the will of the Messiah so that one might see the excellence of his charity and the miracles granted to the saint. When the waves had closed back over him, the parents searched for the child and did not find him. They no longer doubted that he was dead and devoured by sea monsters. They wept for him, performed works of charity for him, and had masses celebrated according to the custom. The following year, when the sea separated again, the people entered as usual * and found the child alive. They asked him: "How did you remain here and what did you feed on?"


[p. 361] {#po.3.p361}
لهم ان القديس كان بطعمنى ويسقينى وبحرسنى من وحش البحر فمجدوا السيد السيح الممجد قديسيه والمستشهدين على اسمه القدوس له المجد دائما الى ابد الابدين ودهر الداهرين امين1
He answered them: "The saint gave me to eat and to drink and protected me against the sea monsters." They praised Our Lord the Messiah who glorifies his saints and those who receive martyrdom in his holy name. Glory to him eternally and in the centuries of centuries! Amen.
اليوم ألثلثين من هتور اعلموا يا اخوة ان فى مثل هذا اليوم قال يقظر انى كنت اعرف رجل قيم فى بيعة على اسم السيدة بقرية من اعمال الغرية وكان قد شاخ وكبر وقدم ولده موضعه وكان للبيعة مال عظيم واوانى وان القيم ادركه الموت ومات بغتة ولم يعرف ولده موضع الاوانى والاموال فاما اسقف الكرسى فانه تقل على الصبى ولد القيم وطاب منه الانية وقال له اذ لم تحضر لى الانية إلى ثلشة ايام والا سلمتك للسلطان وانه بكى بكا مرا وقال لزوحته دعنى اقوم وانطلق الى الشيوخ بشيهات وامير المواضع فانى توجبت الى الرهبان
THIRTIETH OF HATOUR (November 26). Know my brothers, that on this day Victor (Bigtor) said: I knew a man in charge of the administration of a church in the name of Our Lady, in a city of one of the western provinces. He was old and aged and his son was appointed in his place. This church possessed a considerable treasure and precious vases. The old man died suddenly without making known to his son the place of the vases and the treasure. The bishop of this diocese was malevolent toward this young man: he demanded the vases from him, saying: "If you do not present them in three days, I will deliver you to the sultan." The other wept bitterly and said to his wife: "Let me leave and go find the elders of Scetis (Cheihäât) and the chief of those places." I went to find the excellent monks and they


[p. 362] {#po.3.p362}
الفضلاء فقالوا لى لم تصل الى هذا الامر بل امضى فتحن نشيو عليك ان تتمضى الى الرفيق وتجتمع يقفرى الجمال فهو يعلمك بالمكان اما انا فقمت وسرت الى سمنود فوجدت الرجل وله ثلاثة اجمال فضافنى واقمت عند تلك الليلة فقلت له ما اأكل طعام حتى تطلنى على الموضع الذى فيه قماش البيعة فقال لى نهار الغد اقول لك وامضى بسلام وانه اخرحنى الى بيت فى مناخ الجمال لانام فيه وبينما انا نائم سمعت صُوتا رقيقا بسح ! فقمت من البيت الذى نائم فيه وتطلعت فابصرت اثاثة جمال يصلون مع قفرى صاحيهم ويسجدوا لسجوده ويقوموا لقيامه واصرفوا تلك الليلة سهارى ولما كان الصبح تقدم الى الجمال ومسحهم وشد رحاليم لينطلق الى الغيط ليحمل الجمال فقلت له يا ابى انا امضى معك اليوم الى الفيط اساعدك لانى شاب وانت رجحل شيخ ققال لى يا ولدى هذا اليك فركيبت معه واتيت الى موضع فيه كتان مربوط حزما اما انا فاخذت احد الجمال واعتقت فى الوسق واوقرته أكثر من الطاقة وشددت عليه وطلبت ان يقوم فلم يقوم فلم يطيق وتحرك ثلثة دفوع ولم يقدر بثور الحملة فاخذت عصاة وتقدمت. لاضريه واحرقته بالمطرق وانه استغاث بلسان فصيح قائلا يا ابى قفرى لم تركت هذا .يوذينى لانه حمانى ما
said to me: "You have not reached the end of this affair, but go: we advise you to go toward your traveling companion and to join Qarfri the camel driver; he will make this place known to you." For me, I rose and I went to Samanoud; I found the man; he had three camels. He gave me hospitality and I stayed at his place that night. "I will not eat food," I said to him, "until you make known to me the place where the objects of the church are." He answered me: "Tomorrow morning I will tell you; go in peace." He made me go out into a room at the place where his camels were parked, to sleep there. While I was asleep, I heard a sweet voice that praised God. I went out of the room where I was sleeping, I looked, and I saw the three camels that were praying with Qafri their master, prostrating themselves when he prostrated himself and rising when he rose. They spent all that night watching. The next morning, he went toward his camels, rubbed them, attached their saddles to go to the garden to load his camels. "My father," I said to him, "I will go today with you to the garden to help you, for I am young and you are old." - "As you wish, my son," he answered. I climbed with him and I arrived at a place where there was flax tied in bundles. I took one of the camels, I tightened the load, I loaded him more than he could; I wanted to make him rise; he did not rise and could not; three times he stirred without succeeding in lifting the load. Then I took a stick and advanced toward him to strike him, I beat him with blows; but he called distinctly to his master for his help, saying:


[p. 363] {#po.3.p363}
لا طاقة لى به وهوذا انت تبصر وهو يضربنى فخلصنى منه ليلا اطالبك امام الله فقال لى قفرى يا ولدى لم فعات هذا وامرنى ان اخفف عنك' ولذلك كثر تعججى كيف الجمال يتكلموا بلسان فصيح واوسقنا الجمال واتينا الى القربة و حل ارحال الجمال وتقدم ومتحهم بيده واعلفهم وانه قدم لى طعاما وشرابا فأكلت وشربت ورأيت قله ولما كان فى اليوم الرابع سألته عن حاحتى فقال لى احلف باسم الرب يسوع المسيح انك لا تخر بما شاهدت الا بعد وفاتى وانى حلفت له فقال.لى امضى الى بلدك وادخل البيعة الذى لك واعمد الى الجانب الغربى بين البيكل والحلق الصغير فارفع البلاطة التى تتبحت رجلى الشماس فتجد باب المكان وهو مغارة بوسع ما تدور عليه البيعة وفيها ما خلفه ابوك وغيره ممن سقه اما انا فاتيت وعرفت زوحتى بما قد رأايته من القدشين وانى عمدت الى الموضع وفتحته ودهشت لما فيه من- الاموال واخرحت مال البيعة واحضرته امام الاسقف فشاهده وحملته ولما مات الاسقف وكرزوا غيره كنت قد شخت وكبرت واتى مضيت الى انا يوحنا اللطريرك فعرفته بالمال فحضر معى الى البيعة واخرخت له المال
"My father Qafri, why do you let me be mistreated by this one? He has loaded me beyond my strength, and here it is that under your eyes he strikes me. Deliver me from him for fear that I might complain against you before God." Qafri said to me: "My son, why did you act thus? He orders me to rid him of you." Then my astonishment was great to hear the camels speak distinctly. We loaded them and we went to the city. He unloaded them, went to rub them with his hand, gave them fodder, then presented me with food and drink. I ate, drank, and I saw what he was doing. The fourth day, I questioned him about my affair. He said to me: "Swear, by the name of Our Lord the Messiah, that you will not reveal until after my death what you have been a witness to." I swore it to him. He added: "Go into your country, enter into your church; direct yourself toward the left side between the altar and the small partition; lift the slab that is under the feet of the deacon, you will find the door of the place; it is a crypt of the width of the circumference of the church. There is there what your father and others of his predecessors have left." I left; I informed my wife of what I had seen from the saints, I directed myself toward this place, I opened it, and was astonished at the treasures it contained; I withdrew the riches of the church and presented them before the bishop; he saw them and I took them back. When he died and they consecrated another, I had become old and aged; I went to find Anbâ John (Youhannä) the patriarch and gave him knowledge of the treasure. He went with me to the church, I withdrew the riches and handed them to him and my responsibility was


[p. 364] {#po.3.p364}
وسلمته اليه وبريت ذمتى منه فحمله الى مدينة الاسكندرية والمجد لله دائما ايديا سرمديا ! فى هذا اليوم تنيح القديس اكاكيوس بطريرك مدينة القسطنطبنية هذا القديى كان عالما خبيرا مفسرا لمعانى الكتب الالبية فقدم قا على بيعة القسطنطينية ولما اتفق اجتماع المجمع بخلقدونية لم يرض هذا الاب بكلما جرى فيه ولما طلب منه الحضور لاجل علمه احتج بانه مريض ولما جرى على دسقورس البطريرك ما جرى عز على هذا القديس ذلك وتكرهه وبدأ يلعن لاصحابه ومن يق اليه من الوزراء والمقدمين الذى يعرف منهم صحة الايمان وحسن الموافاة اطلعهم على ذلك ثم شكر الله الذى لم يحض معهم ولما مات اناطوليوس بطريرك القسطنطينية اختير هذا الاب من؟ المقدمين والوزراء المومنين العارفين منه صحة الايمان لرتبة البطريركية فتقدم رحا منهم ومنه ان عسى يزيل شيا من الذى جرى وليزيل ما حدث فى البيعة من الشقاق والغضة فلما تقدم ووجد المرض قد استحكم وشفا عسرا جدا رأى ان من الصوب واجل الرأى ان يتم بخلاص نفسه اولا فارسل رسالة الى الاب بطرس بطريرك الاسكندرية يعترف له فيا بصحة
cleared; he transported them to the church of Alexandria (El Iskandaryah). Glory to God continually, perpetually, and eternally! On this day died Saint Acacius (Akäkyous), patriarch of the city of Constantinople (El Qostantinyah). This saint was learned, scholarly, and well-versed in the explanation of the meaning of the divine books. He was placed at the head of the church of Constantinople as a priest. When the Council of Chalcedon (Khalgadounyah) took place, this father did not give his assent to everything that occurred there. When he was asked to attend because of his learning, he feigned illness. When Dioscorus (Disqouros), the patriarch, experienced his misfortunes, it was painful to this saint: he detested the council, cursed its partisans, and made it known to those of the ministers and leaders whose sincerity of faith and loyalty he knew. Then he thanked God that he had not been with them. Upon the death of Anatolius (Anâtoulyous), patriarch of Constantinople, this father was elected by the faithful leaders and ministers who knew the sincerity of his faith to occupy the patriarchate. It was hoped by them and by him that perhaps he would put an end to a part of what had happened and would put an end to the discords and hatreds that had appeared to us in the church. After being enthroned, he found that the evil had strengthened and that its healing would be a very difficult thing; he recognized that there would be no success and decided to occupy himself first with the salvation of his soul. He wrote to our father Peter (Botros), patriarch of Alexandria


[p. 365] {#po.3.p365}
الايمان1 الذى ورثه عن الاباء الالبيين كيرلس وديسقورس ثم اتبعها بعد رسائل يسأله فيها قبوله معه فى الشركة فلما قرأ الاب بطرس رسائله كتب له جوابهم فى رسالة حامعة وارسلها مع ثلاثة اساقفة فتنكروا ودخلوا القسطنطينية واجتمعوا بهذا الاب وسلموا اليه الرسالة التى على ايديهم فاكرمهم اكراما جزيلة وقبل الرسالة منهم احسن قبول وقراها على من يختص به من المومنين من مقدموا الكنيسة واعترفوا جميعهم وهو ايضا بالايمان المستقيم النى تضمنته رسالته ثم كتب اماسهم رسالة بخط .يده جميعبا بقبول الاب ديسقورس والاب طيماتاوس والاب بطرس واعترافه بانهم مستقيمى القلوب ودحص من بيخالفهم ثم اخذهم وخرج الى بعض الديارة وقدس هو واباهم وشاركهم فى القداس والقربان ثم اخذوا منه الرسالة وتباركوا منه وهو ايضا تبارك مهم وودعهم فلما اوصلوا الرسالة للاب بطرس واعلموة شركنهم معه فى القداس قبلا وكرز باسمه فى القداسات والصلوات فاتصل الخر باسناققة الروم فانفوا القديس اكاكيوس من كرسى القسطنطينية وتيبح وهو ثابت على الامانة الارتدكسية صلاته تكون معنا امين
(El Iskandaryah), a letter in which he confessed to him the sincerity of the faith that had been transmitted to him by * the divine fathers, Cyril (Kirillos) and Dioscorus. He followed it with other letters in which he asked him to receive him into communion with him. When our father Peter had read these letters, he addressed the response to him in an encyclical that he sent to him by three bishops. They disguised themselves, entered Constantinople, met with this father, and delivered to him the letter they had in their hands. He rendered them great honors and gave the most beautiful welcome to the letter. He read it before his intimates, among the faithful leaders of the church; all confessed, as did he, the orthodox faith that this epistle contained. Then he wrote one entirely in his own hand before them, accepting (the faith of) our father Dioscorus, of our father Timothy (Timâtäous), and of our father Peter, recognizing the orthodoxy of their hearts and refuting those who were opposed to them. Then he took the messengers, went to a convent, celebrated the holy sacrifice with them, and associated them with him in the mass and communion. They then received the letter, were blessed by him and he by them, and he bade them farewell. When they had brought the epistle to our father Peter and had informed him that he had associated them with him in the holy sacrifice, he received it and placed his name in the liturgies and prayers. The news reached the bishops of Greece (Er Roum); they deposed Saint Acacius from the see of Constantinople, and he died firm in the orthodox faith. May his prayer be with us! Amen.


[p. 366] {#po.3.p366}
! وفيه ذكر مقاريوس الشهيد وفيه تكريز بيعة القديس قزمان ودميان واخوتهم وامهم صلوات الجميع تكون معنا وتحرسنا من ضربات العدو الشرير الى اخر الدهور كلما أمين تم وكمل شهر هتور المبارك بسلام من الرب امين ولله الحمد والشكر على كل حال مين
On this day takes place the commemoration of Saint Macarius (Magäryous). On this day took place the consecration of the church of Saint Cosmas (Qozmân), Damian (Damyân), their brothers, and their mother. May their prayers to all be with us and protect us against the blows of the wicked enemy until the end of all centuries! Amen. The blessed month of Hatour is finished and ended with the peace of the Lord. Amen. To God be glory and praise in all circumstances! Amen.


[p. 367] {#po.3.p367}
بسم الاب والابن والروح القدس الاله الواحد له المجد دائما ابدا امين! شبن كبك المبارك؟
* In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God: glory to him continually and eternally! Amen BLESSED MONTH OF KIHAK
اليوم الاول فى هذا اليوم4 تتيح القديس بطرس الرهاوى الاسقف هذا؟ كان من جنس كريم فاعطوة ابايه لتاودوسيوس الملك ليصير اميرا وكان يرفض لامر ويستعمل النسك والعادة فى بلاط الملك7 وكان معه بعض اجساد القديسين الشهداء الذين9 من الفرس وكان عمرة يومد عكثرين سن وبعد هنا10 خرج وترهب جمل اسققا على اورشليم وما يليها4! غصا وقيل عنه اول قداسه16 فاض دم عظيم آ1 من الجسد الذى كان
(November-December) FIRST OF KIHAK (November 27). On this day died Saint Peter of Edessa (Botros er Rohâoui) the bishop (497-510). He was from a distinguished family; his parents gave him to the Emperor Theodosius (Tâoudousyous) so that he might become a leader. But he neglected his orders and practiced piety and devotion in the imperial palace. He had with him one of the bodies of the holy martyrs who had come from Persia (El Fors). He was then twenty years old. After that, he left and became a monk; then he was made bishop of Jerusalem (Ourichalim) and its surroundings by force. It is said that at his


[p. 368] {#po.3.p368}
قدامه على المذبح! حتى ملاً الصينية3 وكانوا؛ اتوا بجسد يعقوب المقطعة فى دير الرهاوويين فلماة كان فى زمان مرقيان7 هرب الى ارض١ مصر ومعه؟ جد يعقوب المقطع 0! واقام!! فى عض الديارة! ايام وفيماذا هو يقدس فى احد الايام وكان بض الشعب يتحدثواة! فى القداس ولم يكن القديس ينهاهم فرأى ملأك الرب وقد7! مسكه8! من وسطه واراد ان9! يرميه الى السفل لاجل انه90 استحى ان ينشهرهم ووقرهم فلما اتقضت ايام مرقيان عاد الى ارض فلسطين وثنت الليعة4 واتفق اجتماعه بالقديس انا اشعياة المصرى واتصل ضر للملك البار زينون فاشتبى ان يبصره7 فلم يفمل لانه كان يهرب من مجد هذا العالم ومضى الى ناحية من بلاد الغور واقام فيها وكان قد اتفق عيد القديس بطرس !3 بطريرك الاسكندرية
first mass, abundant blood flowed from the body that was before him on the altar, so much so that it filled the paten. The body of James (Ya'qoub) the Intercisus had been brought to a convent of the Edessenes. When it was the time of Marcian (Marqyân), he fled to the land of Egypt (Misr), having with him the body of Saint James the Intercisus, and remained for several days in a convent. One day when he was celebrating the holy sacrifice of the mass, some of the faithful began to talk during the office without the saint preventing them. He saw an angel of the Lord who seized him by the middle of the body and wanted to throw him down because he was ashamed to chase them away and feared them. When the times of Marcian had passed, he returned to the land of Palestine (Falastin) and strengthened the church. He met with Saint Anba Isaiah (Icha'yä) the Egyptian. The pious Emperor Zeno (Zinoun) heard of him and desired to see him, but that did not happen because the saint, fleeing the glory of this world, had gone to a district of the land of El-Ghour and remained there. On the day of the feast of Saint Peter (Botros), patriarch of Alexandria (El Iskandaryah), he celebrated


[p. 369] {#po.3.p369}
فقدس! فى عيده فظهر له القديس وقال له ان السيد السيح قد دعاك فسوف تاتى اليا ومن ذلك اليوم حس بموته فاستدعى الشعب واوصاهم ان توا على الامانة المقدسة1 ويصوا بعضهم بعضا وتبعدوا من العداوة والبغضة والزنا والسرقة والكذب والايمان الحانثة والتجديف والكبرياء والعجب والصلف والنميمة ومحبة النصيب الاوفر والغضب والحقد والدفع بالربا وما اشبه هذه الاشياء واوصاهم بالصلاة لله عنه وقاإل اذكرونى يا اولادى واوصا الكبنة بالتحفظ قائلا لا تغفلوا عن الصلوات والقداسات والبيع الذى انتم مكرزين عليهم انظروا فيهم بعين المصلحة واذا كانوا حاضرين شى من اوانى البيعة كلها مثل كتاب او ثياب اليكل او شى مما تحتاج اليه البيعة فان اليد يسكنه فى بيعة الابكار السمابية ويسمعه الصوث الفرح القائل تعالوا التى يا ماركى ابى اروا الملك المعد لكم من قبل انشاء العالمة ثم بسط .يديه واسلم الروح صلاته معنا امين؟
the mass: the saint appeared to him and said: "Our Lord the Messiah has called you; you are going to come * towards us." From that day on, he felt his death; he brought the faithful and recommended that they remain firm in the holy faith, to love one another, to abstain from enmity, hatred, adultery, theft, lying, false swearing, blasphemy, pride, vanity, boasting, slander, immoderate love of fortune, anger, jealousy, usury, and all such things. He recommended that they pray to God for him and said to them: "Remember me, my children." He recommended that the priests be vigilant and said to them: "Do not neglect the prayers and the holy sacrifice: watch with the concern for improvement over the churches to which you are consecrated, if there are objects belonging to the church such as books, sanctuary vestments, or anything necessary for the church. (He who acts thus), God will make him dwell in the church of the heavenly virgins; he will make him hear this joyful word: Come to me, you blessed of my Father, inherit the kingdom that was assigned to you before the beginning of the world." Then he stretched out his hands and gave up his soul. May his prayer be with us! Amen.


[p. 370] {#po.3.p370}
اليوم الثانى من شهر! كيهبك فى مثل هذا اليوم تنيح3 القديس ابا هور الراهب كان هذا؛ من اهل ابرجتة من الصعيد وكان راهبا” مختارا قد فاق على كثيرين من القديسين فى عادتة وكان محال للانفرادة1 فخرج الى البرية3! قحسد: العدى وظهر له مكاشفة14 وقال له18 اما فى البرية فانك تظبنى بحكم ان ما عندك احدا! ولكن ان كنت شجاع بطل تمال الى الاسكندرية0 ققام !8 واتى الى الاسكندرية وكان يسقى الماء للمجوسين والمنقطعين فاتفق ان خيول كانوا يركضون فى وسط المدينة فصادف احدهم طفلا فضربه فمات !3 لوقته فدخل الشيطان فى اناس يدوا يقولون ما-قتل هذا
SECOND DAY OF KIHAK (November 28). On this day died Saint Abba Hour, the monk. He was a native of Atripe (Abardjat) in Upper Egypt (Es Sa'id) and was an elite monk who, in his piety, surpassed many saints. He loved solitude and left for the desert. The enemy (Satan) hated him; he appeared to him openly and said: "In the desert, you prevail over me for the reason that there is no one with you; but if you are a brave man, a hero, go to Alexandria (El Iskandaryah)." The saint left and went to that city where he carried water to the prisoners and captives. It happened that horses began to run in the middle of the city; one of them struck a child with a blow that killed him on the spot. Satan entered


[p. 371] {#po.3.p371}
الطفل! الا هذا الراهب الشيخ فاتى4 القديس واخذ الطفل واحتشنه وهو يصلى" عليه ويدعو السيد السيح فى قلبه7 ثم رشم عليه8 رشم9 الصليب 9! فرجعت !! نفسه اليه فاعطانا لابويه! وخرج خارجا"! من المدينة فطلبوه! فلم يُجدو فاتى الى دير اا فمبكث ١7 فيه19 فلما قربت نياحته9! ابصر جماعة القديسين يدعوه! ففرح جدا وارسل خلف اولاده واوصاهم وعرفهم انه ينتقل الى السيد المسيح فحزنوا مرض مرضا قلل واسلم مفسة يد الرب صلاته معنا امين وفى هذا اليوم ايضا تنيح القديس انا هرميثة وهذا كان من بلاد البهنسا وابهاته كانوا ابرار أرتدكسيين ولما نشاً فى القامة اخرجوه ليرعى غنم لوالديه فظهر له القديس .يوحنا
among the people and they began to say: "This child was killed only by this old monk." The saint arrived, took the child, and pressed him to his chest while he prayed over him and invoked in his heart Our Lord the Messiah; then he made the sign of the cross over him and his breath returned. He returned him to his parents and left the city. They looked for him, they did not find him; he went to a convent where he remained. When his death approached, he saw a troop of saints who were calling him. He rejoiced greatly, sent for his (spiritual) sons, gave them recommendations, and informed them that he was going to join Our Lord the Messiah. They were afflicted by it. Then he was ill for a short time and gave up his soul into the hands of the Lord. May his prayer be with us! Amen. On this day also died Saint Anba Hermina; he was from the city of Pemdije (El-Bahnasä). His parents were virtuous and orthodox. When he had grown up, they sent him to guard a flock that belonged to them. Saint John


[p. 372] {#po.3.p372}
وضيخته عظيم الرسل بطرس ودعوة الى السيرة المطيهرة النيرة اعنى اسكيم الرهينة فاطاعهم وصعد معهما الى دير انا يتغوب وهو كان اب الدير فى ذلك الزمان وطلبوا امن مام الصبى عنده وتعليمه سنن الرهنة فقبلهم عنده واحضر لهم عدة الرهبنة فصلوا عليها والبسود الاسكيم المقدس فحت عليه النعمة ونزل كاروييم من السماء ومعه قريانة فجلها على المذبح وحضر داوود النبى المرتل وقرأ من المزمور ما يواقف وكذلك الرسول بولس لان العطر حضر وقرأ ما يوافق ويوحنا قرأ الانجيل وبطرس قدس وتقربوا من السرائر المقدة ومُكنُوا بروضوة ويشتونا فى السلوك فى العادة ويسلموة لاا بوب اب الدير وانصرفوا ولما كان بعد اياما وهو متابر على النسك والجهاد ظيهر له يوحنا وعرفة ان يذهب الى الجيل التقلى فحضر اليه انبا هور المعروف بالقصار وساروا اثنييهما معا الى الل القلى فوجدوا نخلة وبر ماء فمكث هناك اياما وطاف على الاصفياء واتتقل الى الجل الذى شمال مدينة قاو والمكان الذى اوعده الرب ان فيه يكون كمال جهاده وبشيع اسمه وتذكارة هناك الى جيل الاجيال فكان يجاهد بنسك عظيم وشظف ويضرب فى
(Youhannä) appeared to him in the company of the chief of the Apostles, Peter (Botros); they called him to the pure and brilliant life, that is to say, the monastic habit. He obeyed them and left with them for the convent of Anba James (Ya'qoub) who was the superior at that time. They asked him to keep this young man and to instruct him in the rules of the monastic life. He welcomed them into his home, presented the monastic objects: they prayed over them and clothed the young man in the holy habit. Divine favor descended upon him. A cherub (Käroubim) came from heaven, carrying an offering that he placed on the altar: David (Dâoud) the prophet, the psalmist, also presented himself and sang the appropriate psalms; likewise the apostle Paul (Boulos), with the fragrant tongue, recited what was necessary; Saint John read the Gospel; Peter officiated. They approached the holy mysteries, began to encourage the young man and to strengthen him in the way of the service of God, handed him over to Anba James, the superior of the convent, and left. After a few days, while he had exercised himself in devotion and struggle, John appeared to him and warned him to go to the southern mountain. Anba Hour, nicknamed the Fuller, presented himself to him and they both left together for the southern mountain. They found a palm tree and a well of water: the saint remained there and visited the saints. Then he went to the mountain that is to the north of the city of Qâou; it is there that, according to the promise of the Lord, his struggle was to end: his name would spread and his memory would live there from generation to generation. He practiced great devotions, underwent privations, and prostrated himself


[p. 373] {#po.3.p373}
الليل الف ومايتى مطانوة ومثلبا فى النهار فظهر له الشيطان عدو جنسنا وطلب من الرب فارسل له ملأكه وزجر وولى هاربا بغضب عظيم وللوقت اطلق سافى رمل فاردم القديس والخزاة فطلب من الرب فارسل اليه ملأكه وقض الشيطان وكلفه عمل الرمل وجعله نظف الموضع جميعة ولما ظفر بعدوا وقهر شبح برجليه على بر ماء ووقف عليها ثابتا ويداد مبسوطتان اربعين .يوما متوالية وهو صائم حتى بيسن جمده ونزل ملأك الرب ورفع نفسه وحمله ! وقربه واطعمه من اشجار الفردوس وعاده الى مسكنه وقد اشرق وحبه كموسى رئيس الانياء ولما اراد ان يكمل سعيه فحضر انا هور وأا _يوساب واخوة اخرين الجبل فاوصاهم بان يواروا جسده وللوقت اتى الرب وميكايل وغبريال ورفاييل رووساء الملائكة واوعده بالخيرات السماية وكل من يذكره ويعمل تذكاره وبعد هذا اسلم روحه فى يد الرب ورتلوا امامه الملائكة الى ملكوت السموات وحضروا اهل مدينة قاو واخذوا بركتة وكفنوا جند وقدسوا علية وتقربوا ودفنوا .جسلدا الى
twelve hundred times at night and as many during the day. The enemy of our race, Satan, appeared to him: the saint asked for help from God who sent him his angel. The demon was chased away and fled with great fury. He immediately sent a cloud of sand that entirely covered the saint and his cell. The latter asked for help from the Lord who sent his angel; he seized Satan and forced him to carry away the sand and to clean this place completely. When he had defeated and tamed his enemy, the saint rested his feet on the well of water, stood there constantly, hands outstretched, for forty consecutive days; he fasted so much that his body withered. An angel of the Lord descended, took his soul, transported him, and fed him with the (fruits of the) trees of Paradise, then brought him back to his dwelling. The face of the saint shone like that of Moses (Mousâ), the chief of the prophets. When he wanted to put an end to his efforts, Anba Hour, Anba Yousäb, and other brothers from the mountain went to him: he recommended that they bury his body, and immediately the Lord arrived with Michael (Mikäyil), Gabriel (Ghabryâl), and Raphael (Rafäyil), chiefs of the angels. They promised him heavenly goods as well as to anyone who would mention him and make his commemoration. Then he gave up his soul into the hands of the Lord and the angels sang hymns before him until the kingdom of heaven. The people of the city of Qâou presented themselves, received his blessing, put his body in the shroud, heard the mass before the body, received communion, and buried him next to


[p. 374] {#po.3.p374}
جانب الببعة وظهر من جسده ايات كثيرة وايضا فى كل الكنائس المنية على اسمه بكل مكان ! مجدا لله وقديسيه الرب يرحمنا بصلاته امين البوم الالك من شهر كيبك
the church. Numerous miracles appeared where his body was, as well as in all the churches built under his invocation in every place. Glory to God and to his saints! May God have mercy on us through his prayer! Amen. THIRD DAY OF KIHAK (November 29).
فى مثل هذا اليوم كان دخول العذراء3 السيدة والدة الاله3 مرتمريمة الى اليكل يروشليم وهى انة9 ثلاثة سنين لانها7 كانت نذرا لله8 لان" امها حنة لمالا كانت بغير ولد !1 وكات معودة من النسوة فى هيكل الله وكانت *! حزينة جدا نا هى والشبخة! الكريم9! يواقيم بعلها”! سمع الله تنهدهما وانذرت لله نذرا ان الثمرة التى يعطيبها ليها تجعلها لله فلما رزقت الست السيدة الطاهرة18 ربنها19 ثلشة سنين فى بيئنها وبعد ذلك مضت بها مع الجوارى ! الى اليكل فاقامت فى البيكل الى عشر سنة
On this day took place the entry of the Virgin, Our Lady, Mother of God, Mary (Maryam), into the temple of Jerusalem (Yarouchalim), when she was three years old, * for she had been vowed to God. Indeed, her mother Anne (Hannah), having no child, was kept apart by the women in the temple and was very afflicted by it, she and the venerable old man Joachim (Youâgim) her husband. God heard their sighs; she pledged to consecrate to him the fruit that he would grant her. When the Lord granted her Our Lady immaculate, she raised her for three years in her house. Then, she brought her to the temple with the


[p. 375] {#po.3.p375}
تمل ! الطعام من" الملائكة الى حيث الوقت الذى ياتى فيه الرب" الى العالم ويتجد من" هذ التى اصطفاها حينشذ اشتور" الكبنة ان19 يودعوها عند من يحفظيا لاثها نذرا للرب ولا يستقيم لها!! ان ييقوها فى البيكل! ليلا يحصل لبنا ما يحصل للنساء ثم سيموالا عليها اسم الخطية! حتى تحل له ان بعانيهاة! فجمعوا من سبط .يهودا انثى عشر رجلا ! اتقياء لينظروا من يودعبها وآأحذوا عصيهم ودخلوهم الجيكدل فانت حمامة ووقفت بوداعتها على عصاةة24 يوسي فعلموا ان هذا تا الامر من الله ولانه كان صديقا بارا فتسلمها وبميت عنده الى حين اتى ليها الملاك جبراييل بشرها تجد الابن مها شفاعتها معنا امين
virgins, and she remained there for twelve years, receiving her food from the angels until the approach of the moment when the Lord was to come to this earth and be incarnated from her, whom he had chosen. Then the priests consulted to know to whom they would entrust her to guard her because she was vowed to the Lord, and it was not possible for them to leave her in the temple for fear that what happens to women might happen to her. They inscribed for her the name of the request for marriage, so that she could lawfully be protected. They gathered from the tribe of Judah (Yahoudzä) twelve pure men to examine who would take care of her; they took their staffs, placed them in the temple. A dove came to rest on the staff of Joseph (Yousof). It was recognized that this indication came from God, for he was a just and virtuous man. He received her and she remained in his house until the angel Gabriel (Djibrâyil) came to find her and announced to her that the Son would be incarnated from her. May her intercession be with us! Amen.


[p. 376] {#po.3.p376}
اليوم الرابع من شهر كيهبك فى هذا اليوم استشهد القديس الرسول اندراوس اخو بطرس هذا كان قد خرج سهمه ان يمضى"الى4 لد والى بلادة الاكراد فلما دخل" مدينة لد كان. اكثرها قد امن على يدى بطرس8 وكان معه" تلميذه فيليمون9! وكان فليمون !! شجى الصوت! حكيم محمن المنطق فامر14 ان يصعد الانبل وبشرأً فلما سمعوا17 كنة الاوثان بسجى التلميذ اخذوا الة18 حرابهم واتوا"! البيعة يتتصتوا ان كانوا يشتموا الهتم فلما سمعوا فيليمون! يقول ان البة الامم ذهب وفضة لها افواه لا تنطق واعين لا تنظر واذان لا تمع فليكن صانعها ال فمن حسن ونه انحلث قلوبهم ودخلوا الببعة
FOURTH DAY OF KIHAK (November 30). On this day died as a martyr the holy apostle Andrew (Andrâous), brother of Peter (Botros). The arrow he had shot indicated to him to go to Lydda (Lodd) and to the Kurds (El Akräd). When he entered this city, the greater part of the inhabitants already believed through the intermediary of Peter. He had with him his disciple Philemon (Filimoun), who had an imposing voice, wisdom, and beautiful language. Andrew ordered him to mount the pulpit and read. When the priests of the idols learned of the coming * of the disciple, they took their weapons and came to the church to listen if the apostles would insult their gods. When they heard Philemon say: The gods of the nations are of gold and silver: they have mouths, and do not speak; eyes, and do not see; ears, and do not hear; may he who makes them be like them. Their hearts opened because of the beautiful voice of Philemon; they entered the church, fell


[p. 377] {#po.3.p377}
وخروا! تحت قدمى؟ التلميذ اندراوس وآمنوا بالسيح فوعظيم التلميذ! وعندهم هم وجماعة من عابدى الاوثانة ثم خرج من عندهم" واتى الى بلاد الاكراد ومدينة اكسيس " ومدينه ارجناس " ومدينة اسيفوس9! بعد ما كان قد مضى مغ برتلوماوس ١! الى مدينة عازرينوس؟! وما جرى لهم من رأس4! الكلب حتى اتوا الى معرفة الله فلما دخل هذه المدينة ونادى فيهم وكانوا لا يعرفون”! الله18 اشرار جدا قليلى الطاعة فأمن بعضهم لاحل الايات والعجائب التى 9! راوها وبقوا الذين لم يومنوا توامروا عليه بمشورة ردية فيما بينهم وارسلوا"ة خلفه بمكر حتى حضر ل" اليهم فيوثوا عليه ويقتلوه واوائك الرسل لما اتوا اليه وسمعوا !* تعليمه الحسنة ورأواء ببحة وجبهة3
at the feet of Andrew and believed in the Messiah. The disciple exhorted them and baptized them, them and a troop of idolaters. Then he left them and went into the land of the Kurds in the city of Scythia (Aksis), in that of Achaia (Ardjenäs), and in that of Asifous after he had gone with Bartholomew (Bartoloumâous) to the city of Azerinous and they had adventures with the Cynocephali, until they came to the knowledge of God. When he entered this city (Patras) and preached there, the inhabitants did not know God and were extremely wicked and disobedient. A part of them believed because of the wonders and miracles they saw. But those who remained unfaithful conceived evil designs against him. They sent after him by ruse so that he would present himself to them: then they would rush against him and kill him. When these envoys reached him, heard his beautiful teachings, and saw the radiance of his face, they


[p. 378] {#po.3.p378}
آمنوا بالمسيح ! ولم يعودوا الى الذين ارسلوهم فتقالوا" نقوم نحن بانفنا وناخذد ونحرقه فاجتمعوا+ جماعة كثيرة واتوا اليه فسأل السيد المسيحة قنزل نار" من السماء واحرقنهم فخافوا الاقية جدا7 وشاع ذكر ذلك التلميذ فى جميع" تلك البلاد وامن بالرب خلق كثير ومع هذا لم يكفوا" كببنة الاوثان عن طلب ل التلميذ وانهم اجتمموا كلهم واتوا واخذوا التلميذ !! وضربوه ضربا كثيرا! وطافوا به المدينة”! عريان والقوذ فى السجن حتى اذا كان بالغداة صلبوه وكان عادتهم اذا قتلوا واحد " فانهم ياخذوة وبصلود!! على عود ويرحمود وفى تلك الليلة سأل ٠" التلميذ 9! المسيح ان7! تتزل ارا من السماء وتحرقهم كالدفعة"! الاولى فظيهر له السيد المسيح وقال له لاتقلق ولا تضجر فقد قرب انصرافكم من هذا العالم !# واعطالا السلام وغاب عنه فابتتهجت نفس القديس التلميذ !3 فلما كان باكرا اخذوه وصلوه على خشة ورحموة بالحجارة حتى ة تيح
believed in the Messiah and did not return to those who had sent them. The infidels said: "We will go ourselves to take him and we will burn him." They gathered in considerable number and went to him. He implored Our Lord the Messiah; a fire descended from heaven and consumed them. The rest were very frightened: the reputation of this disciple spread in all these lands and a very numerous people believed in the Lord. Despite this, the priests of the idols did not renounce pursuing the disciple: they all gathered and came to seize him; they struck him with many blows, paraded him naked through the city, and threw him into prison. The next day, they put him on a cross. It was their custom, when they killed someone, * to take him, put him on a cross, and stone him. That night, the disciple asked the Messiah that a fire descend from heaven and consume them as the first time. Then Our Lord appeared to him and said: "Do not be troubled and do not be in anguish, for your departure from this world is near." He gave him salvation and disappeared. The soul of the holy disciple was rejoiced. The next day they took him, crucified him on a beam, and stoned him with stones until he died. Then


[p. 379] {#po.3.p379}
فانوا! اقوام مومنين واخذوا جسده المقدس ووضعوة فى قبر وظيهر منه ايات كثيرة وعجائب باهرة" صلواته تكون معنا الى الابيد امينة اليوم الخاسن من شهر كيهبك
faithful people came to take his holy body, placed it in a tomb; numerous miracles and striking wonders manifested themselves there. May his prayers be with us eternally! Amen. FIFTH DAY OF KIHAK (December 1).
فى هذا الوم ذكر الصديق البار ناحوم النبى هذا من قيلة سمعون وهو فى التبى7 البادس عشر من موسى انبى وهذا البى تن فى زمن اموصيا ابن يوناداع ويسمى بواش وفى زمن عوزيا ولده وبكت بنى اسراييل على كفرهم وعادتهم للاصنام وابان فى نوته ان الله تعالى رحوم طويل الاناة كثير الاناة الا انه لا بد ان ينتقم من اعداه وشنانه ويحفظ لهم العذاب وتنا على بشارة الانجيل وعلى التلاميذ المبشرين بها وسماهم منادبين بالخيرات ومشرين بالسلامة وتباً على مدينة نينوى وانها سوف تنظر من الميلا ومن النار وكان كذلك وتم لان الله جعل فيها زازلة فاثارت نارا واحرقت بعضها وهو الث
On this day takes place the commemoration of the just and pious Nahum (Nähoum) the prophet. He was of the tribe of Simeon (Sim'oun) and was the sixteenth in the order of prophecy since Moses (Mousâ). He prophesied in the time of Amaziah, son of Joash (Youâch), and in the time of his son Uzziah ('Ouzyâ). He reproached the Israelites for their infidelity and their idolatry, explained in his prophecy that God is merciful, patient, full of long-suffering, but that he necessarily punishes his enemies and those who hate him; that he reserves a punishment for them. He also prophesied the preaching of the Gospel and the disciples who would announce it and named them those who proclaim good things and who announce salvation. He prophesied about the city of Nineveh (Ninoui), what it would experience from water and fire: this happened and was realized, for God produced an earthquake there from which gushed a fire that consumed


[p. 380] {#po.3.p380}
النى رجع عن طريق العدل وارتكب الاثم والقوم الذى كانوا تابن لله لم يناليم بؤى ولا صابهم ضر ولما اكمل نبوته وارضى الله بسيرته تنيح " بسلام_بركاته تحفظنا امين
a part of it; these were the scoundrels who had turned away from justice and had committed iniquity. As for the people who repented, no evil reached them and they had to suffer no damage. When he had finished his prophetic mission and satisfied God by his conduct, he died * in peace. May his blessings
! اعلموا يا اخوة انه فى مثل هذا اليوم استشهد القديس بقطر وهذا كان من بلاد اسيوط شرقى البحر وكان جندى فى قصر شو ولما انوا بمرسوم دبقلاديانوس وفيه مكتوب هكذا ان يسجدوا للاصنام ويرفعوا لهم اللبان ولما انشهت القرعة الى هذا الصبى بقطر فاعتفى وكان عمره انتى عشر سنة وكلمه مقدم القصر باللطف فلم يقدر عليه فامر ان يلقوه فى السجن فلما سمعوا ابهاته حضروا اليه وشجعوا قلبه على الشهادة فاخرحه مقدم القص وسأله ثانى مرة ولم يقدر عليه ولا يرد قله عن امانة السيد المسيح فللوقت غضب عليه وكتب رسالة الى الامير الكبير بمدينة اسيوط وعرفه بما جرى منه وسيره مع اناد الى اسيوط ولما قرأ الامير الرسالة واوقفوا قدامه قال له عرفنى ما بالك خالفت مقدم القصر فان سمعت منى اعطيتك منزلة كبيرة عالية واكتب للملك من احلك ان انت قيلت
keep us! Amen. Know, my brothers, that on this day died as a martyr Saint Victor (Bigtor). He was from Syout (Osyout), east of the river, and served in the fortress of Chaou. When the rescript of Diocletian (Diglädyânous) was brought, thus conceived: "Let one prostrate oneself before the idols and offer them incense," and when the turn of this young man arrived, he refused: he was twelve years old. The commander of the fortress spoke to him with gentleness, but he could not convince him and he ordered him to be thrown into prison. At this news, his parents came to encourage his heart for martyrdom. The commander of the fortress had him brought out and questioned him a second time, without succeeding, nor turning his heart from the belief in Our Lord the Messiah. Then he became irritated against him and wrote a letter to the chief governor, in the city of Syout, informing him of what had happened and sending him Victor guarded by soldiers. Having read the letter, the governor said to Victor: "Tell me why you resisted the commander of the fortress? If you listen to me, I will give you a considerable and high position and I will write to the king about you. If you obey me, he will grant you the government


[p. 381] {#po.3.p381}
منى يعطيك الولاية على احد المدن فصرخ القديس باعلا صوته وقال مملكة هذه الدنيا زائلة والذهب يتغير والثياب تملى وحسن الجسد يفسد ويتدود وبّلاشا فى القبور ولا يجب لى ان اترك عنى سيدى سوع المسيح خالق السموات والارض ورازق كل ذى جد واعبد الاوثان الحجارة مسكن ابليس فغضب عليه وامر بان يعذب بانواع العذاب فلم يقدر يغير قلبه عن اماتته ثم انه امن بان يربطوه خلف الخل ثم انه صار مصعد الى قرية تسمى ابسيديا وهنلك اعرض عليه السجود للاصنام فلم بطيعه فامر ان يقضى عليه ويكتب قضيته فان يلقى فى مستوقد الحمام فى قرية تسمى موشة شرقى قرية اسيديا فطلب من الاجناد ان يطلقوه يصلى فسأل الرب الاله فظهر له ملك الله واوعده بمواعيد كثيرة وبالخيرات الكثيرة الدائمة فى ملكوت السموات وكلمن يفعل! له تنكار في يوم نباحته يكون حالس فى مقام الالف سنة وكلمن يكون فى ضيقة والذين فى الشدايد والحار اذا ما ذكروا اسمك فالمعونة تدركهم سريعا والذى يكتب شهادتك انا اكتب اسمه فى سفر الحياة ثم ان الصبى القديس مارى بقطر المبارك التفت الى الاجناد وقال لهم اكملوا ما قد امرتم به فربطوه ورموا فى مستوقد الحمام فأكمل صبرا الحسن وشبادتة
of a city." Then the saint cried out at the top of his voice: "The kingdom of this world is perishable, gold tarnishes, clothes wear out, the beauty of the body spoils, is prey to worms and disappears in the tombs; I must not abandon my Lord Jesus (Yasou') the Messiah, creator of heaven and earth, dispenser of food to every being, nor worship idols of stone, dwellings of the devil (Iblis)." The irritated governor ordered him to be subjected to all sorts of torments, but he could not turn his heart from his faith. Then he had him tied behind horses and went up to a city named Ibsidyâ. There, he proposed to him to prostrate himself before the idols. The saint did not obey. He ordered him to be judged and his sentence written, condemning him to be thrown into the furnace of the baths in a city called Mouchah, east of the city of Ibsidyâ. The saint asked the guards to let him pray; he implored the Lord God. An angel of God appeared to him, made him many promises, and announced to him many perpetual goods in the kingdom of heaven: that whoever would make his commemoration on the day of his death would sit in the place of the thousand years: "Whoever (he said) will be in distress, whoever will be in danger and at sea, if he invokes your name, help will come to him quickly; whoever writes your martyrdom, I will write his name in the book of life." Then the young saint Mar Victor the blessed turned to the guards and said to them: "Finish what you have been ordered." They bound him and threw him into the furnace of the bath. This is how


[p. 382] {#po.3.p382}
السعيدة ونال الاكليل فى الملكوت فاتوا اناس مسيحيين فى الخفا واخذوا جده واخفوه الى ان اقلب الله كرسى ديقلاديانوس عليه واعمى بصرة وقطع خبره وشهدوا الذين وجدوا جد انا نظرئلا سالما ولم تحترق فيه شعرة واحدة بل كان كمثل الانسان النائم فبنوا عليه كنيسة عظيمة وهى باقية الى الان فى قرية موشة وظهر منها ايات وعجائب كثيرة وهودا عجائه طاهرة الى يوم الناس هذا الرب يرحمنا بصلاته امين! اليوم السادس من شهر كيهك
ended his beautiful constancy and his happy martyrdom: he received the crown in the (heavenly) kingdom. Christians came in secret to take his body; they hid it until God overturned the throne of Diocletian upon him, blinded his sight, suppressed his mention. Those who found his body rendered this testimony: "We saw him intact, not a hair was burned, but he was like a man asleep." A great church was built for him that still subsists in the city of Mouchah, and to our days, numerous miracles and wonders occur there. May the Lord have mercy on us through his prayer! Amen. SIXTH DAY OF KIHAK (December 2).
اعلموا يا اخوة انه فى مثل هذا اليوم انه كان شيخ عظيم من القديسين حائز دفعة على ففدق ففنظر صبية حميلة زانية فدخل اليها يخلص نفسبا فاحتج ان يفعل بها فقال لها الشيخ انا اخاف هنا فادخاته الى داخل سبع خزائن وهو فى كل واحدة يقول ليا انا اخاف فلما تعمت قالت له يا شيخ انت تتلاها بى لعلك من الله تخاف قال ليا الشبخ
Know, my brothers, that on such a day, there was a respectable old man among the saints who passed once near an inn: he saw a beautiful young woman who was giving herself to prostitution. He entered her place to save her soul, and pretended to want to have commerce with her. The old man said to her: "Here I am afraid." She made him enter the interior of seven cells, and at each one he said to her: "Here, I am afraid." When she was tired, she said to him: "Old man, you are mocking me; perhaps it is God you are afraid of. - One would say that you


[p. 383] {#po.3.p383}
وكانك تعرفى ان الله كائن ولما قال هذا صار مثل من .يريد يخرج ليمضى فامسكته قائلة لنت اخليك الا ان تاخذنى معك لتخلص تفسى فقال لها الشيخ بحق تجى فقالت له انا اجى فقالت هنا ذهب كثير اتريد ان اخذ معى ققال لها الشيخ ان كنتى تريدى فقط ان تيخلصى نفسك وتتوبى فما ذا تصنعى بالذهب ولما سمعت هذا قلعت الزبون الاخر من رحليها رمته وجعلت تمشى خلف الشييخ حافية لانها فى الفندق اولا لا يزال الزبون فى رجليها فى كل وقت فلما مشت فى الطريق مع الشيخ لكونها لم تتعود ان تمشى حافية تفاحت رجليها وسال مهم الدم وهى تمشى وكانت تصبر وتشكر الله ولما كان المساء وهم يمشون فى الطريق اتوا الى شجرتين فقال لها الشيخ امضى تحت هذا الشجرة وارقدى وهو ايضا مضى تحت الاخرى رقد ولما كان فى تلك الليلة رأى الشيخ مناما كانه مسك حمامة ففرت من" يديه وصعدت الى السماء ولما كان الصباح مضى الشيخ ينها ليسيروا فوجدها قد تتبحت فلما وجدها ميتة انزعج قائلا يا سيدى خرجت هذا لتتوب وتتعد لاسمك فلم تمكنها ان تصل لتتوب فجاء اليه صوت قائلا لا يتجع قابك فقد فرغت ان تكمل توبتها فان كنت تشاء ان تعلم انها قد كملت توبتها بالحقيقة وتطيب
know that God is present," said the old man, and he acted as if he wanted to leave to go away. She held him back by saying to him: "I will not let you go until you take me with you to save my soul. - Will you really come? he asked her. - I will come." - Then she added: "There is a lot of gold here, do you want me to take it with me? - If you only want to save your soul and repent, what will you do with gold?" At these words, she took the shoes off her feet, threw them away, and began to walk barefoot behind the old man, for previously in the inn she did not cease to have shoes on her feet continually. When she walked on the road with the old man, as she was not used to walking like this, her feet cracked and blood flowed, but she walked with constancy and praising God. Evening having arrived, while they were on the road, they came near two trees. The old man said to her: "Go under this tree and lie down." He himself went to sleep under the other. That night, he had a dream: it seemed to him that he had taken a dove and that it had fled from his hands and had gone up to heaven. The next morning, he went to wake the woman to set out on the road, he found her dead. At this sight, he was troubled and said: "Lord, this woman had left to repent and serve your name; it was not possible for her to arrive." A voice said to him: "Do not let your heart be afflicted: she has succeeded in doing penance entirely. If you want to know that her repentance has been really complete, and calm your
قلبك على توبتها فارجع الى الطريق التى حيتما فيها وائتما تمشيان معا فانت تجد توبتها فى المسلك فلما رجع الشيخ الى ملك الطريق كما امر الله وحجد الطريق مرشوشة دما من النقط الذى تقطت من رحليها لانها كانت رطبة الجسم وكانت حجارة الطريق تقطع رجليها وهى صابرة على التعب ولم ترجع الى خلف لانها كانت عازمة على التوبة وتتخلص من اجل هذا قبل الله منها عزيمتها بسلام الرب الاله يرحمنا الجميع بصلاتما أمين
heart on this subject, return to the path that you followed while walking together: you will find her repentance on the road." He returned on this path, as God had ordered him, and found it watered with blood because of the drops that had flowed from her feet, for she had a delicate body; the stones of the road had torn her feet: she had endured the fatigue with constancy and had not turned back, because she was decided to do penance and to save herself; thus God had accepted her project in peace. May the Lord God have mercy on us all through her prayer! Amen.


[p. 384] {#po.3.p384}
! فى هذا اليوم نعيد لتذكار القديس الشهيد اناطس_القس وفيه ايضا تنيح الاب القديس ابانا ابراهام هذا الاب كان من نصارى المشرق وكان تاجر ذو مال كثير وقد تردد الى مصر عدة مرار ثم سكن بها اخيرا وظهرت منه فضائل ورحمة وشاع ذكر بن بالصلاح والعلم فوقم اتفاق الاساقفة والمشائخ العلماء عليه ان يقدموه بطريركا على مدينة الاسكندرية فلما تقدم فرق كلما له على الفقراء والمساكين وقطع كل عادة ردية من سائر كرسيه ومنع واحرم كل رئيس ياخذ من احد شيا لاجل تقدمه فى
On this day we celebrate the commemoration of the holy martyr Anatolius (Anatis) the priest. On this day died our holy father Ephrem (Abrâhâm); this Father was descended from Eastern Christians; he was an excessively rich merchant. He had come a great number of times to Egypt (Misr), then he settled there at the end. His generosities and his mercy manifested themselves; his reputation for virtue and science spread: the bishops and doctors were in agreement to raise him to the rank of patriarch of the city of Alexandria (El Iskandaryah) (920-975). When he had been enthroned, he distributed everything he possessed to the poor and the unfortunate. He suppressed every bad custom in the extent of his see: he forbade and defended any superior from receiving anything from anyone on the occasion of his elevation to a rank in the church; he forbade


[p. 385] {#po.3.p385}
الي ومنع من ! يتشد سرية وتشدد فى ذلك كثيزا 'فلما سمعت ارباب السرارى منعه خافوا الله تعالى ونخافوا من حرمه فاخربجوا جميعهم سراريهم واتوا اليه وتابوا بين يديه فقبل توشهم وغفر لهم ما تجروا عليه سوى رجحل واحد كان صاحب ديوان له حاه عظيم من الدولة هذا المسكين لم بيخاف الله تعالى ولا حرم هذا الاب فوعظه كثيرا وطول روحه عليه وضرب له عدة مطائيات فما ارتدع ولا خشى ان يهلكه الله وهو ييصر ,تلك الشية المقدسة مرمية على الارض بين رحليه المستحقة للقطع وبعد هذا لم يترك تعليمة له واصلاحه بل اتضع كالمسيح خالقه وحاء الى داره ولما سمع ذلك الكافر بمجى هذا الاب الى داره غلق الباب فمكث الاب قدر ساعتين قائم على بابه وهو يقرع الاب فلم يفتح له ولا كلمه فلما تحقق " هذا الاب ان هذا المسكين قد افصل نفسه بيده من رعية المسبح وقد صار بجملته عضوا فاسدا وان هذا الاب لم يبق عليه يبه اثم ولا لوم رأى من الصواب ان يقطعه من الجسم ليلا يفسد بقية الاعضاء فجيل دم على رأسة واحزمه ونفض غار نعله على بابه النجس فاظهن الله ايته فى تلك الساعة امام اعين
anyone who took a concubine, and on that, he showed himself excessively severe. When those who had concubines learned of it, they feared God and the excommunication of the patriarch, sent away all their concubines, and came to find him to do penance before him. He accepted their repentance and forgave them what they had committed. Only one made an exception: it was a government official who occupied a considerable situation in the State. This wretch did not fear God Most High, nor the excommunication of this Father. The latter exhorted him much, showed patience toward him, and made numerous supplications for him; but the culprit did not let himself be brought back and did not fear that God would make him perish, although he saw this holy old man lying on the ground before his feet which would have deserved to be cut off. Despite this, the patriarch did not renounce instructing him and correcting him, but he humbled himself like the Messiah his Creator. He went to his house. Upon learning of the arrival of this Father, this infidel locked himself in. The patriarch remained two hours, standing, before the door, at which he was knocking. The other did not open for him and did not address a word to him. When this Father was certain * that this wretch was cutting himself off from the flock of the Messiah and that he had become totally a spoiled member without himself having to reproach himself regarding him for a fault or a cause of blame, he deemed it fitting to cut him off from the body, so that he would not spoil the rest of the members. He cast his blood upon his head, excommunicated him, and shook the dust of his sandal on his soiled door. At that moment, God made his miracle appear to the eyes of the


[p. 386] {#po.3.p386}
الحاضرين فانشقت عتبة الدار على اثنين وكانت صوان فيا للعجب ان الحجر الصوان انشق لسماع الحرم وقلب ذلك الرحل المملو اثام لم يلين بعد ذلك اظيهر الله فيه عليم اياته فانه افتقر الى ان لم ببق له درهم فرد ثم نفى من خدمته مانا وابتلى فى جسمه بإمراض صعة ومات شر موته وصار عبرة لكل احد وارتدعت به خطاة كثير وخافوا مما نابه وفى زمن هذا الاب كان للمعز1 وزير وكان يهودى واسلم وكان له صديقا يبودى وكان يدخل به الى المع فى أكثى الاوقات ويتحدث معه فتمنا اليهبودى بجلا الوزير على المعز ان يحضر له الاب البطريرك ليجادله فحضر هذا الاب وصحبته انا ساويرس ابن المقفع وجادلا اليبودى واخجلاه وانفصلا بمعونة المسبح غالبين مكرومين من المعز فلم يحتمل اليهبودى ولا الوزير ذلك وبعد ايام دخل الوزير على المعز وقال له تريد تعلم يا مولانا ان النصارى ماهم على شى فى انجيلهم يقول" اذا كان لاحديركم امانة مثل حبة خردل فانه يقول لهذا الجل اتقل فينتقل فاستحضر الطريرك واطلب منه ان يقوم بما قاله انجيله فان لم يقم بالدليل فاعلم انهم على " غير شى فاستحضر
assistants: the threshold of the house split in two, although it was a hard stone. O wonder! this hard stone split upon hearing the excommunication, but the heart of this man filled with sins was not softened. Then, God made one of his great wonders burst forth: this wretch became so poor that he did not have a single dirham left, and was, by contempt, deprived of his position; he was struck in his body with violent illnesses and he died the worst death. This was an example for everyone: many sinners were frightened and feared what he had experienced. In the time of this Father, El-Mo'izz had a vizier who, from a Jew, had become a Muslim. He had a Jewish friend whom he very often brought to El-Mo'izz to converse with him; this one desired, by the credit of the vizier, that our father the patriarch be brought to dispute against him. He appeared, and with him Anba Severus (Sâouiros) ibn El-Mogaffa'. They argued against the Jew, confounded him, and, thanks to the help of the Messiah, separated victorious and honored by El-Mo'izz. Neither the Jew nor the Vizier supported this defeat. A few days later, the vizier entered to the prince: "Do you want to know, master, that the Christians do not conform to their gospel? He says: If one of you has faith, as big as a grain of mustard, and if he says to this mountain: Move yourself, it will move. Have the patriarch come and order him to move the mountain according to the word of the Gospel; if it does not move at his indication, know that they do not rely * on


[p. 387] {#po.3.p387}
المعز هذا الاب واعرض عليه هذا القول فسأله ان يمهله ثلثة ايام فاسهله فلما خرج جمع! الرهبان والاساقفة القريين منه ومكثوا بكنيسة المعلقة بمصر ثلثة ايام يصومون ويطلبون من الله فلما كان فى سحر اليلة الثلثة ظهرت له الست الشيدة والدة الاله واعلمته بانسان دباغ قديس وانه الذى يفعل الله هذ الاية على يده فارسل الاب واستحض ذلك الرجل واخذ معه جماعة الكنبنة والرهبان وسائر المومثين وحضروا ين يدى المعز وخرج المعز وكل دولته واهل مصر والقاهرة فوقف الاب البطريرك فى جانب ويقف المعز وباقية الناسن فى جانب اخر ثم صلى البطريرك والمومنين وسجدوا ثلاثة سجدات وكل سجدة يرفع الاب رأسه ويصلب على الجل فيرتفع الجبل قدام الحاضرين ثم اذا ركع ينزل الجبل الى موضعه هكذى ثلشة دفعات فصاحت المسلمون وتعجوا وخافوا خوفا عظيما واستحضر المعز الاب اللطريرك واكرمه كرامة عظيمة وسأله ان يتمنى عليه فلم يرض ان يطلب منه شيالا فاريد عمارة الكنائس وبخاصة كنيسة القديس ابو مرقوره التى بمصر فكتب له منشورا بعمارة الكنائس ودفع له من بيت ماله مال
anything." El-Mo'izz had this Father come and exposed the question to him. The other asked him for a delay of three days, which he obtained. When he had left, he gathered the monks and the neighboring bishops and they remained three days in the church of El-Mo'allagah in Misr, fasting and imploring God. At the dawn of the third night, Our Lady, the Mother of God, appeared to him and taught him that there existed a holy tanner through whose intermediary God would accomplish this miracle. The Father sent to look for him and, this man having arrived, he took with him the crowd of priests and monks and all the faithful and they presented themselves before El-Mo'izz. This one went out with all his court and the inhabitants of Old Cairo (Misr) and Cairo (El-Qâhirah). Our father the patriarch stopped on one side; El-Mo'izz and the rest of the people, on another side. Then, the patriarch and the faithful prayed and prostrated themselves three times. Each time, he raised his head and made the sign of the cross toward the mountain. This one rose before the spectators; then, when he made a genuflection, it descended to its place: this happened three times. The Muslims shouted, were stupefied, and felt a great fear. El-Mo'izz had the patriarch come, showed him great regard, and invited him to express a desire. He refused... "I ask to rebuild the churches and especially that of Saint Abu Margourah in Old Cairo." The prince wrote for him a diploma relative to the construction of the churches and gave him a sum of money from the public treasury. The patriarch thanked him, made


[p. 388] {#po.3.p388}
فتشكر له ودعا له كثيرا وسأله ان يعفى عنه فى ان يترك المال فازداد عند المعز محة لما رأى من ورعه وقلة شرهه ثم ركب معه ووقف فى عمارة برسم الكنيسة التى لابو مرقوره لما تعرض بعض اناس ووقف واقف الى ان نى فيا وجدد هذا الاب يع كثير بسائر الاعمال ولما كمل سعيه " الصالح تنيح بالسلام صلواته تكون معنا امين
many wishes for him and asked him to allow him to leave this sum. Al-Mu'izz felt only more affection for him upon seeing his fear of God and his selflessness. Then he rode on horseback with him and oversaw the reconstruction of the church of Abu Margourah because several people were opposing it. He remained there until it had been built. This Father restored many churches in all the provinces, and when his virtuous task was accomplished * he died peacefully. May his prayers be with us! Amen.
السابع من شهر كيهك !اعلموا يا اخوة انه فى مثل هذا اليوم استشهدوا القديسين الفضلاء النساك انا باينا وباناوة فى زمان ديقلاديانوس وهذا القديس بانينا من اهل دورة سريان وابهاته كانوا نصارى خائفيين من الله وكانت والدته من اهل انصنا فلما اننا هذا القديس بغير تعليم فى بيت ابهاته قالت والدته لابوه انت تعلم بان اخوتى فى انصنا وما اشتهى لهذا الصغير ان يبقى هكذا سائب فان اردت فانا اوديه لاخواله وهم يرسلوة الى المعلم مع اولادهم
SEVENTH OF KIHAK (December 3). Know, my brothers, that on this same day the generous and devout holy martyrs Anba Banina and Anba Naou (Banaou) died in the time of Diocletian (Digladyanous). Saint Banina was from the people of Terot Saraban (Dourat Saryan); his parents were Christians and feared God; his mother was from Antinoe (Ansina). When this saint had grown up without instruction in his parents' house, his mother said to his father: "You know that my brothers are in Antinoe: I do not want this child to remain thus idle; if you consent, I will take him to his uncles; they will send him to a master with their children and they will watch over


[p. 389] {#po.3.p389}
ويحفظوة كمثانا مع منها واخذ الطفل ووالدته وعدوا البحر من دورة سريان الى انصنا وعرفت اخوتها! من اجل الصبى ففرحوا كثيرا واخذوه وودوة الى عند معلم ماهر فتعلم العلم حيد فحسده العريف الذى له لما راد زاكى قالع ناحج اكثر منه وكان يترصد حتى خرج المعلم يوم من الايام فقام بد وغيرة شيطانية واخذ اللوح من يد الصى باينا" وحذفها الى بعيد واكدرها وامسك اصابعه التى يكتب بيهم والوام وكسرهم الى خلف لاحل رطوبة جسد الطفل وهرب لوقته وعدا البح الى قرية كان لوالدته فييها معرفة واختفى عندهم فاما الصبى الطاهر فغشى عليه ولما فاق لعقله بكى بكاء شديدا بمرارة ومضى الى بيت اخواله ولم يعود من ذلك اليوم الى الكتاب وبات تلك الليلة بوجع شديد قاتلا ملاك الرب ميكائيل وشفا أصابعه وقام من يومه كانه لم يتالم البتة وعدى عند ابهاته فوحجد صبى خائف من الرب انا ناو وترافقوا مع بعضهم فى الصلاة والصوم والطهارة فلما نظر الرب الى حسن عبادتهم الزكية ارسل اليهم ميكابيل وامرهما ان يمضوا الى بلاد الفيوم ويسكنوا عند قديس فاضل هناك وسيرته مشهورة البر فاجابوا
him as we do." He listened to her, took the child and his mother, and they crossed the river from Terot Saraban to Antinoe. She informed her brothers of her plans for the child; they rejoiced greatly, took him, and led him to a capable master. || progressed much in science. The monitor, to whom the saint was entrusted, became jealous of him upon seeing his virtue, his successes, and his triumphs which surpassed his own. He watched him until one day, the master having gone out, this monitor rose, filled with envy and a diabolical jealousy, took the tablet from the hands of Banina, threw it far away, broke it, seized the fingers with which he was writing, bent them backward, and broke them, because of the delicacy of the child's body. Then he fled immediately, crossed the river to go to a small village where his mother had acquaintances with whom he hid. As for the innocent child, he fainted. When he had regained his senses, he wept bitterly from pain, went to his uncles' house, and from then on, never returned to school. || spent that night suffering greatly. The angel of the Lord, Michael (Mikayil), came to find him and healed his fingers; he was as if he had never suffered and returned to his parents. He found a child who feared the Lord, Anba Naou; they associated to fast, pray, and lead a pure life. When the Lord saw the beauty of their upright devotion, He sent Michael to them and ordered them to go to the Fayoum and to live there with an eminent saint whose virtuous conduct was


[p. 390] {#po.3.p390}
الملاك قائلين نحن صبيان ولم نلك قط طريق فقال لهم انا اصحكم واوصلكم وتبعود للوقت وسار بهم الى عند ذلك الاب وظهر ملك الرب للقديسن وعرفه بوصولهم اله فخرج وتلقاهم بفرح عظيم وقالهم وسكنوا عنده ثلث سنين وهم يتعلموا من حسن عادته وبعد هذا ظهر لهم رئيس الملائكة ميكايل وقال لهم قد جاء الوقت الذى تنتقلوا فيه من هذا البلاد الى الجبة القبلية كما امر السيد المسيح له المجد فشعوه وهو يمشى قدامسهم وهم ماشيين الى بلاد ابصاى من اعمال اخميم وصعدوا الى الجبل ووجدوا هناك ناك وسواح كثير وكانوا يتقربوا فى يعة لطيفة! لم تسعهم قشطوا السواح وبنوا كنيسة اكبر من تلك وطلبوا الاسقف يحضر يكرزها وكان خوف ديقلاديانوس مبسوط على الديار المصرية وكان الاب الاسقف انا ابسادةة مختفى ومتكر لا يعرفه احد اذا مشى ثم استقاروا السواح يما بيلهم قائلين من هو يذهب يحض لا الاسقف يكرز الكنيسة قهض انا نينا ونزل وكان يطوف فى طاب الاسقف يكرز الكنيسة ولم بنضرة أحد عنه بشى وبعد تعب كثير وهو سائر وحد القديس انا ابسادة فعرفه
famous. They replied to him: "We are children; we have never traveled." - "I will accompany you," he said; "I will make you arrive." They followed him immediately and he accompanied them to this Father. The angel of the Lord appeared to this saint and informed him of their arrival. The other went out, went to meet them with great joy, embraced them, and they lived with him for three years, instructing themselves by the beauty of his devotion. After this time, the chief of the angels, Michael, appeared to them and said to them: "The time has come to transport you from this country to the southern region, as Our Lord the Messiah, glory to Him, has ordered." They followed him; he walked before them and they arrived in the country of Psoi (Absai), in the government of Chmin (Akhmim); they climbed the mountain and found ascetics and devout people there in great numbers. They offered the holy sacrifice in a pleasant church that could not contain them. The devout hastened to build a larger one and asked the bishop to come and consecrate it. At that time, the fear of Diocletian was spread throughout the land of Egypt. Our father, the bishop Anba Psoti (Absadah), was hidden and disguised: no one knew him when he passed by. The devout consulted each other, saying: "Who will go and fetch the bishop for us so that he may consecrate the church?" - Anba Banina left, embarked, and wandered here and there, looking for the bishop to consecrate the church. No one could provide him with information about him. After many fatigues, as he was walking, he found Saint Anba Psoti and recognized him in


[p. 391] {#po.3.p391}
بالروح واستدعاا وقال له ما تريد فعرفه فاظيهر له نفسه ومشى معه بفرح الى الجبل ودخل الى البيعة وكرزها وقبض انا ثنا! وكرزه قسيسا ورفقه انا ناو كرزه شماس وفرح الشعب معهم واقام عندهم ايام وانصرف واما القديس فاقام زمان هو وزفيقه وهم فرحين بعادتهم الزكية وبعد زمان ظهر لهم ملك الرب وامرهم بالانتقال من هناك الى جبل ادريية وكان هناك فى ذلك الجبل صنم وعلى يده صحن نحاس يساع اردب بالكيل وكانوا كبنة الاصنام يعملوا لذلك الصنم عيد فى ثمانية عشر من بابه ويجتمعوا له خلائق لا تحصى من كل الاماكن وياتوا بصيان وعمرهم من اننى عشر سنة الى ما دون وعددهم انتى عشر صبى ويذبحوهم الكبنة فى ذلك الصحن ويعملوا العيد واذا ما هم اصحوا فلا يجدوا من ذلك الدم شى فعند ذلك يفرحوا ويقولوا ان الهم قد قبل قراينهم وياخذوا للوقت اجاد الاطفال ويدفنوهم ويعود كل واحد الى موطنه وهم فراحا قائلين ان الهههم ينجب الزرع فى تلك السنة ولما مكثوا القديسين متعبدين لله فى ذلك الجلى ظيهر لهم ملأك وامرهم ان يصلوا ويطلبوا من الله من اجل ابطال ذلك الصنم فلما ذبحوا الاطفال فى ذلك العيد وصبوا الدم فى الصحن وكانوا القديسين وقوف للصلاة على تل
spirit. He called him, and the other said: "What do you want?" He informed him of it; the bishop unveiled himself and left joyfully with him for the mountain. He entered the church and consecrated it. Then he seized Anba Banina and ordained him priest; he also conferred the diaconate on his companion Anba Naou: the faithful rejoiced with them. The bishop remained several days with them, then he left. As for the saint, he remained for some time as well as his friend, taking pleasure in serving God holily. Then the angel of the Lord appeared to them and ordered them to move from there to the mountain of Atripe (Adribah). There was an idol there that held on its hand a copper basin with the capacity of an ardeb as a measure. The priests of the idols celebrated a feast on the 18th of Bâbeh in honor of this image; an innumerable people gathered there from all sides and brought children whose age was twelve years and under, and the number was twelve. The priests slaughtered them over this basin and celebrated the feast. If in the morning they found nothing of this blood, they rejoiced and said that their god had accepted their sacrifices; they immediately took the bodies of the children, buried them, and everyone returned to his dwelling with joy, saying that their god would grant them beautiful harvests this year. When the saints had remained to worship God on this mountain, an angel appeared to them and ordered them to pray and ask the Lord to destroy this idol. On the day of the feast, when the children had been slaughtered and their blood had been poured into the basin, the saints were standing, praying on a hill to


[p. 392] {#po.3.p392}
جيل غربى ادريية وان الدم الذى كان فى الصحن لم يتغير ثانى يوم كعادة عوائدهم النجسة ففضوا الكبنة وقالوا ما دام هولاء الشيوخ الجليليين فى هذه الناحية فالهنا غضان علينا ولا يقبل ذبائحنا ولا ينجب الزرع فى هذه السنة واذا ما دعونلا فى شدائدنا فلا يستجيب لنا حينئذ اشتدت اربعين غلام من عاد الاصنام لله السلاح ومضوا يطلبوهم بسرعة فسترهم السيد المسيح منهم ومن بعد ذلك لما سمع مكسيمياثون فى ديار مصر نصاردى مخفية فطلع بكر عظيم حتى وصل الى مدينة اسوان وهو يطوف البرارى ! ومعابد الاوثان بكل مكان وكان يدفع لكل بربا ققطار ذهب وكان معه أصنام مزغرفين بحلى الذهب ولاس الحرير ومنهم من يكون فى ايديهم سيوف مسلولة واخرين يكون فى يديهم شروخ وكانت جميع البة الملاهى فى الحراريق قدامهم ولما وصل المنك الى ادرية لينظر برابيها ويفتقد حالهم تقدموا اليه كهنة ذلك الصنم واستفاتوا اليه واشتكوا القديسين وبما حرى لهم فى الذبائح والدم الذى لم يقبله صنمهم بسب الشيوخ القديسين فامر للوقت باحضارهم سرعة فظهر لهم ملأك الرب ميكاييل وقال لهم الملك يطلبكم عجلوا فان الجياد ما هو فى كلوقت مسوط وعرفيهم بما ايجرى عليهم وعزاهم
the west of Atripe. The blood that was in the basin did not disappear the next day, as was the custom of their abominable feasts. The priests became irritated and said: "As long as these Galilean old men are in this region, our god will be angry with us and will not accept our victims; he will not give beautiful harvests this year; when we implore him in our misfortunes, he will not listen to us." Then forty young men from among the idolaters took their weapons and set out in all haste in search of them. Our Lord the Messiah hid them from them. After that, Maximian (Maksimyanous) learned that there were hidden Christians in Egypt (Dyar Misr). He left with a considerable army until he arrived in the city of Syene (Aswan), visiting temples and idol sanctuaries everywhere and giving a gantar of gold to each temple. || had with him statues adorned with gold necklaces and silk garments; some held naked swords in their hands, others sabers; before them were all kinds of musical instruments in boats. When the emperor arrived at Atripe to visit his temples and examine their condition, the priests of this idol went to find him, spoke extraordinary words to him, and complained about the saints and the troubles they had caused them because of the victims and the blood that their idol had not accepted, because of the two saintly old men. || ordered them to be brought immediately. The angel of the Lord, Michael, appeared to them and said to them: "The emperor is looking for you: hasten, for the struggle does not take place at all times." He made


[p. 393] {#po.3.p393}
واوعدهم بالاكاليل السمايية فاتوا الى الملك فوجدوا الرسل فى الطريق فامسكوهم واحضروهم قدام الملك فشتموه وقالوا له ان اصنامك حجارة وخشب باطلة وانهم شياطين وانه غضب عليهم واخذهم صحته وانحدر الى ان وصل الى بركة قالة اتفة فجلسوا فامر الملك باخذ رؤوس القديسين فصلوا عند ذلك وطلبوا من الرب المعوئة فظيهن لهم ملأك الرب وقواهم واوعدهم بالخيرات الحسنة وكلمن يذكر اسمايهم على ,الارض اذا كان فى ضيقة او فى شد بخلصه الرب بصلاتهم وشفاعتهم واخذت الاجناد رؤوسهم بحد اليف ونخمسوا سيوفهم فى تلك البركة الماء فظهر من ذلك الماء ايات وعجائب واشفية ولا سيما الذين بهم مرض المثاثة اذاما استحموا من ذلك الماء بامانة يشفوا واما اجسادهم فقت ! مطروحة حتى انصرف الملك وعسكره فاتوا انان مسيحيين ممختفيين فى تلك التواحى وحملوا الاجتاد وكفنوهم ودفوهم فى الجل قالة تلك البركة ولما بطل الاضطياد بنوا لهم المومنين بيع على اسماءهم وجعلوا اجسادهم فيها وهى الان تظهر فيها الايات والاشفية بركة هولاء الابرار الشهداء تكون معنا ومع الناسخ امين
them know what was going to happen to them, encouraged them, and promised them heavenly crowns. They went to find the emperor and, on the way, met his envoys who seized them and presented them to Maximian. They insulted him and said to him: "Your idols are only useless stone and wood; they are demons." He became irritated against them, took them with him, and went down the river until he arrived at a pond in front of Edfu (Atfah) where they stopped. There, he ordered the heads of the two saints to be cut off. They prayed and asked for the Lord's help. His angel appeared to them, strengthened them, and promised them beautiful rewards: if someone mentioned their names on earth, the Lord would deliver him, thanks to their prayer and intercession, when he found himself in pain or distress. The guards cut off their heads with their swords, which they then dipped into this pond; the water produced miracles, wonders, and healings: those especially who suffered from tertian fever were healed when they bathed in it with faith. As for their bodies, they remained lying there until the departure of the emperor and his army. Then came Christians who were hidden in this region; they took their bodies, put them in a shroud, and buried them in the mountain in front of this pond. When the persecution had disappeared, the faithful built a church in their name and placed their bodies there. Even today, miracles and healings are seen there. May the blessing of these virtuous martyrs be with us and with the copyist! Amen.


[p. 394] {#po.3.p394}
!وفى هذا اليوم ايضا نياحة الاسقف القديس الفاضل الكامل العالم الطاهر انا يوحنا اسقف مديئة ارمنت وهذا القديس ابهاته من اهل ارمنت وكانوا نجادين فى صناعتهم فخرج اخوة الكبير الذى يدعى اسمه العظيم يسنتاوس ومضى الى قصر طود ومكث هناك اياما فظبهرله فى ذلك الموضع شرف الدين النصرانة فاتى الى مدينته وتعمد هو واخوته اننا يوحنا وانا درمتاوس وصعد الى حل مدينته وترهب هناك وعمل عبادات كثيرة فاما هذا الاب القديسى انا يوحنا فانه خرج ذات يوم الى الحرحة ليحطب حطا فاتت عليه افكار من الشيطان جسدية وللوقت طرح نفسه فى وسط الشوك فامتلاً جسده كله من الشوك وتجرح فلما رجع الى الدير عرف القديس انا يسنتاوس بالروح بالذى جرى له فتلقاد وقال له مرحا بالحشى الصغير فهوذا قد اوهبك الرب لاجل احتمالك وصبرك وقوة قللك ورمى نفسك فى الشوك اسققية مدينة ارمنت وبعد تلك الايام طلعوا اهل المديئة الى عند الاب يسنتاوس وسألوه ان يصير اسقف فمتنع من ذلك ودفع لهم اخوة انا يوحنا فاخذوه وذهبوا به الى اسكندرية وكرزوه اسقفا ولما حاء الى المدينة عمد كثيرين من الوثنيين الذين كانوا فى المدينة وكان ينى الكنيسة فى الشهار فياتوا الوثثيين ويهدموها
On this day also occurred the death of the holy bishop, the virtuous, the perfect, the learned, the chaste Anba John (Youhanna), bishop of the city of Ermont, from where his parents were. They were carpenters by trade. His elder brother who bore the venerable name of Pisentios (Bisantaous) left for the castle of Toud; he remained there for several days during which the nobility of the Christian religion appeared to him. Then he went to his city and was baptized there with his brothers, Anba John and Anba Dermataous. Then he went to the mountain of his city, became a monk there, and gave himself up to many devotions. As for this saint Anba John, he went one day into the forest to look for wood. Carnal imaginations were sent to him by Satan: immediately, he threw himself into the midst of the thorns; his whole body was filled with them and torn. When he returned to the convent, Saint Anba Pisentios learned by the spirit what had happened to him. He went to meet him and said to him: "Welcome to the young man stuffed [with thorns]! Behold, because of your patience, your endurance, the strength of your heart, and the courage with which you threw yourself into the thorns, the Lord has given you to be bishop of the city of Ermont." Some time later, the inhabitants of this city came to find Father Pisentios and asked him to be their bishop. He refused and handed over his brother Anba John to them. They took him, led him to Alexandria (Iskandaryah), and he was consecrated bishop. Arrived in the


[p. 395] {#po.3.p395}
فى اللبل فاما هو فلم يضجر من ذلك بل صبر حتى ظيهرت فيهم الايات ورجعوا اليه وطلبوا منه الغفران وعمدهم وكان شديد فى كلامه وقاطع بكلام الحق ولم يتناول قط رشوى شرطوية فى قسمه ولا هدية بل كان يقول مجانا اخذتم مجانا اعطوا وانه انحدر دفعة الى القلاية فارست المركب على ساحل المدينة وطلع الى البر ونظر اناس. معلقين باذرعتهم عن الخراج فقال من هو الذى جسس وعلق صورة الله فعرفوا ان الوالى الذى علقهم ثم انه مضى سريع الى دار الولى فقرع باب الدار بعجلة عظيمة حتى ان الامير كان حالس يتغدا فمن شدة الضرب فى الاب فدفع كلما كان فى موادة فقالوا له ان استقف من الصعيد فاستدعلا وقال له يا ابونا تجس على دار الولاية هكذا فقال له الاسقف انا صنعتى تجار اما الباب اذا انكس انا اقدر اصلحه واما صورة الله اذا تغير فيها شى ما تقدر تجعله مثلما كان الى الابد فقال له الامير يا ابونا السلطان يطالبنا بالخراج فما تقدر تترك لاحد شى فقال له الاسقف قبل ان يسافر! وان الاراحة كانوا
city, he baptized many of the idolaters who were there. By day, he built a church and, by night, the pagans came to destroy it, but he did not let himself be troubled: on the contrary, he took patience until miracles appeared. Then the idolaters returned to him, asked him for forgiveness, and he baptized them. || was energetic in his language and decided according to justice. He never received gifts for the laying on of hands, nor presents, and he said: "Freely you have received; freely give." Once, he went down to El-Qalayah: the boat dropped anchor near the shore of the city and he went ashore. He saw people suspended by their arms because of the tax. "Who has dared to act thus and suspend the image of God?" he asked. He was told that it was the governor. He went quickly to his dwelling and knocked on the door with great haste, so much so that the governor, who was sitting at lunch, let fall what was on the table, because of the violence of the blows. He was told: "It is a bishop from Upper Egypt (Es-Sa'id)."
